## Runner's own moments (player view, iPad, persona rebel/rule-breaker) — the LONGEST life of E-S3: 23 years, 3792 buổi
- y1-y23 · BORED · rank 0→1→0 for 23 years (Đáp ứng at best); the middle is the same pages («Kỳ nguyệt sự» Báo lùi vài hôm / Báo đúng ngày / Đặt lại, «Biếu quà làm lành», «Tin báo» …) hundreds of times; a rule-breaker finds no rule worth breaking and no path up
- y19-y23 · FRUSTRATED · THE SAVE WALL: the game save reached 5.2 M chars (≈ browser localStorage quota) at buổi ~2714 / y19: from then on every setItem failed with QuotaExceededError and the stored save froze (SAVE STALE: stored b2714 vs live b3751, ~1040 buổi not saved); the player sees nothing — the game plays on while a crash/reload would drop ~3 years of life; also «ctm-nr» (25 K) failed to save
- y23 end · ODD/DELIGHTED · «Xuân tàn lãnh cung — Tuổi xuân qua đi» at 40 (game-imposed); the end black card is covered by an item-detail popup «Nến mỡ dê / Thường / Nến» (a salary-chest item opened by the harness' tap on the chest line) with «Tiếp theo» faded behind it — popup over the end card, 13th life · shots-R2-rebel-5/end.png
- whole life · FRUSTRATED · 13 tab crashes (renderer memory), 8 builds crossed on resume
